Activation still requires an independent linguistic or contextual trigger.","semantic_detail":{"applicability":"Maddi çekirdeğin tamamını, hem selin taşıdığı karışık parçaları hem de sıvı yüzeyine çıkan düşük değerli artıkları birlikte belirtmek için uygundur.","boundary_detail":"Dal, bulantıyı, bitkinin kurutulmasını ve insanlar için kullanılan küçültücü benzetmeyi değil, sıvıda taşınan ya da yüzeye çıkan maddi döküntüyü anlatır.","branch_image_ar":"غثاء يطفو ويحمله السيل","concept_gloss":"akışla taşınan veya sıvı yüzeyine çıkan döküntü","contextual_glosses":[{"applicability":"Selin kuru bitki, kumaş parçası ve benzeri maddeleri sürükleyip su yüzünde topladığı bağlamlarda kullanılır.","error_profile":{"adds":null,"collision":null,"fit":"none","loses":null,"preserves":"Selin taşıyıcılığını, su yüzündeki konumu ve döküntünün karışık maddi yapısını korur."},"facet_ids":["F001"],"text":"selin taşıdığı yüzey döküntüsü","usage_role":"contextual"},{"applicability":"Köpük ve kuru bitki parçalarının suyun ya da tenceredeki sıvının yüzüne çıktığı bağlamlarda kullanılır.","error_profile":{"adds":null,"collision":null,"fit":"none","loses":null,"preserves":"Yüzeye çıkma hareketini ve sıvıda beliren düşük değerli madde niteliğini korur."},"facet_ids":["F002"],"text":"sıvının yüzüne çıkan artık","usage_role":"contextual"},{"applicability":"Suyun yaprak, kamış, hayvan pisliği ve benzeri parçalar bakımından çoğalıp kirlenmesini anlatan yapıya bağlı kullanım içindir.","error_profile":{"adds":null,"collision":null,"fit":"none","loses":null,"preserves":"Döküntünün suda çoğalmasını ve suyu dolduran karışık madde niteliğini korur."},"facet_ids":["F003"],"text":"döküntüyle dolmak","usage_role":"explanatory"}],"definition":"Selin taşıdığı ya da bir sıvının yüzüne çıkıp dağılan, kuru bitki, köpük, kumaş parçası ve benzeri düşük değerli maddelerden oluşan döküntüdür. Buna, suyun yaprak, kamış ve hayvan pisliği gibi maddelerle dolması da bağlı bir kullanım olarak katılır.","distinctive_facets":[{"facet_id":"F001","role":"core","statement":"Selin taşıdığı veya su yüzünde yüzen kuru bitki, kumaş parçası ve benzeri değersiz maddelerden oluşan döküntüdür."},{"facet_id":"F002","role":"extension","statement":"Tencerenin ya da başka bir sıvının yüzüne çıkan köpük ve kuru bitki parçaları da bu adlandırmanın kapsamına girer."},{"facet_id":"F003","role":"associated_use","statement":"Suyun hayvan pisliği, yaprak, kamış ve benzeri döküntülerle dolması, aynı maddi görüntüyü kuran yapıya bağlı bir kullanımdır."}],"identity_rationale":"Kaynak ifadesi, selin taşıdığı veya suyun ve tencerenin yüzüne çıkardığı kuru bitki, köpük, kumaş parçası ve benzeri değersiz döküntüleri aynı maddi çekirdekte toplar. HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_03dc44c41feb62a1d94a","text":"3. **Low-value people/things as moral metaphor.** Lexical evidence: غثو B004 unregarded refuse; شقو B002 hardship in 87:11; دنو B003 baseness in 87:16. Structural cue: the later avoidant \"الأشقى\" and preference for الدنيا follow the vegetation/refuse cycle. Assigned role: the pasture's fate becomes a moral analogy for choosing the low and perishing. Abductive move: the packet gives metaphorical refuse and later moral failure; I infer analogy from sequence. Reading change: the ayah becomes a warning about human valuation, not only natural decay.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000374/B006","root_001073/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_111e73823cbfd6e07b98","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:5:2\",\"root_001073\",[\"B004\"]],[\"87:5:3\",\"root_000374\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Karanmış artık, 87:14'teki arınmanın karşı sürecini görünür kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2a60f8e64f9c6e010ca3","text":"4. **A darkened opposite to purification.** Lexical evidence: حوي B006 dark coloring; سبح B002 declaring free/purifying; زكو B002 purity/rectitude in 87:14. Structural cue: darkened refuse stands before later purification and prayer. Assigned role: أحوى provides the visual negative of تزكى. Abductive move: the packet supplies darkening and purity; I infer contrast as a moral-visual mechanism. Reading change: the ayah sets up purification as refusal to become dark residue.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000248/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_36d5f1482d587bba284b","text":"1. **The verse describes controlled phase conversion.** `ج ع ل/B002` is turning something into a state, `غ ث و/B002` is pasture dried or reduced into chaff-like refuse, and `ح و ي/B006` supplies the dark or blackish coloring of `أَحْوَىٰ`. The pronoun reverses the emergence of pasture in 87:4, and `فـ` binds input to result. Each branch has a distinct role: transformation, material endpoint, and visible condition. I infer an ordered lifecycle rather than accidental spoilage. The ayah reads as deliberate state change, not a static picture of debris.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3d31b87aa912471833cc","text":"87:14 made the refuse image morally sharper: تزكى is not only piety but a counter-process to becoming غثاء. 87:18-19 also reframed this transience as an old recorded lesson, not merely local observation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001073/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4495a36a0495b1d92b41","text":"The later recitation/reminder sequence in 87:6 and 87:9–10 produced the largest surprise. `غ ث و/B004` and the cast-off branch `ن س ي/B003` turned vegetal refuse into a material analogy for selective retention: the ordered system discards one output while preserving and reactivating another.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001073/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_451b334c932454dc5ab3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:5:2\",\"root_001073\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Değersiz sayılan artık, yakın dünya hayatına verilen yanlış değerin maddi benzetmesine dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_67bc7b500354a6eb9405","text":"2. **Containment and gathering in decay.** Lexical evidence: حوي B001 gathering/holding, B002 coiling/rounding, B008 enclosed water catchments; غثو B001 refuse gathered on water. Structural cue: refuse is not only dry; it is gathered and carried. Assigned role: أحوى may activate enclosure/aggregation around the waste. Abductive move: the packet supplies water-surface refuse and holding basins; I infer that decay collects what once grew. Reading change: the image becomes a hydrological sorting: life emerges, then remnants are gathered into dark scum.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000374/B001","root_000374/B002","root_000374/B006","root_000374/B008","root_001073/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6b84cf1aa46b67514cc3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:5:2\",\"root_001073\",[\"B001\"]],[\"87:5:3\",\"root_000374\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B006\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Çerçöp, yalnız kurumuş bitki değil, suyun taşıyıp karanlık bir tabaka halinde topladığı artık görüntüsü kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000248/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_712eb9091d5a9b2a7b74","text":"1. **The green yield becomes flood refuse.** Lexical evidence: ج ع ل B002 (turning into a state), غ ث و B001/B002 (flood refuse and dried pasture chaff), ح و ي B006 (dark blackish coloring). Structural cue: direct pronoun returns to المرعى in 87:4; fa- marks transformation. Assigned role: جعل executes state-change; غثاء names the reduced material; أحوى gives the darkened end-state. Abductive move: the pasture's final visible form reveals the instability of worldly growth. Reading change: the ayah becomes the ecological argument later stated ethically in 87:16-17.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_72d0b50f770c0adc74e8","text":"1. **Green provision turned into flood-refuse.** Lexical evidence: جعل B002 turning something into a state; غثو B001 floating refuse carried by floodwater and B002 pasture dried into chaff-like refuse; حوي B006 dark reddish/blackish coloring. Structural cue: the pronoun returns to المرعى of 87:4 and the فـ makes transformation immediate. Assigned role: جعل performs state-change; غثاء names the degraded residue; أحوى marks darkening. Abductive move: the packet supplies pasture and refuse; I infer decay after growth. Reading change: the ayah reads as the lifecycle of worldly provision, not a separate punishment scene.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001073/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_76ec2d4984692a3540f9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:5:2\",\"root_001073\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Artık olarak atılma görüntüsü, hemen sonraki unutmaya karşı korunan okuma ile seçici bir tutma ve dışarıda bırakma karşıtlığı kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000374/B006","root_001073/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a2a6db407e3b3f2e7fcb","text":"2. **Decay redistributes and gathers rather than simply erases.** `غ ث و/B001` gives refuse floating or carried by floodwater, while latent `ح و ي/B001` gives gathering and holding; the surface adjective still retains its direct color support in `ح و ي/B006`. The branch image of low material collected one layer over another supplies the structural bridge between the two roots. I infer transport and accumulation from the accepted refuse image, since no water is stated in the ayah. The result becomes a dark gathered residue whose form has changed but whose matter remains.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000374/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b0776da2a5e971421cf6","text":"3. **Containment turned dark.** Lexical evidence: ح و ي B001/B002/B006 (gathering/holding, coiling, dark coloring), ق ر ء B001 (gathering speech), ص ح ف B003 (collected sheets). Structural cue: the root ح و ي can imply containment, while later recitation/sheets gather meaning. Assigned role: أحوى is a dark gathered mass, contrasting with orderly gathering. Abductive move: not every gathering preserves value; some gathering is waste accumulation. Reading change: the ayah distinguishes decomposing collection from scriptural/recitative collection.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_da29561c193d5f359706","text":"87:6 made غثاء feel like the physical image of نسيان: what is not held in ordered recitation becomes cast-off matter. 87:17's أبقى then made the chaff an explicit foil for what remains.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001073/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e7246a0f6e69e62c2892","text":"2. **Discarded memory versus preserved recitation.** Lexical evidence: غ ث و B004 (unregarded refuse), ن س ي B003 in 87:6 (forgotten cast-off thing), ق ر ء B001/B002 (gathered recitation), ب ق ي B001/B002 in 87:17. Structural cue: the verse about chaff is followed immediately by \"We will make you recite, so you will not forget.\" Assigned role: غثاء provides the negative image of what happens when form is not preserved. Abductive move: forgetting is a cognitive analogue to biomass becoming refuse. Reading change: 87:5 prepares the anti-forgetting promise.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001073/B004","root_001501/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e965a7fdc8d5a71b7282","text":"3. **Material discard becomes a model for informational sorting.** `غ ث و/B004` is what is unregarded and allowed to go to waste. In 87:6, `ن س ي/B003` supplies the forgotten cast-off thing, while 87:9–10 activates `ذ ك ر/B009` and `ذ ك ر/B003` as reminder and recovered presence. The fixed refuse therefore plays the rejected-output role against protected recitation and recollection. I infer a material-to-cognitive analogy; the packet does not equate vegetation with discourse. The ayah gains a contrast between what an ordered process discards and what the next sequence preserves.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
